{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLaUFM49B7VjexCojaKf","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PDwFi52bAmjqx3mKxq6qBB","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T083506Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/src/lib/usdc.ts","content":"import { erc20Abi, getAddress } from \"viem\";\nimport { mainnet } from \"wagmi/chains\";\n\n/**\n * Canonical USDC (Circle) contract on Ethereum mainnet.\n * https://etherscan.io/token/0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48\n *\n * `getAddress` returns the checksummed form and throws at build/import time\n * if the literal is ever mistyped.\n */\nexport const USDC_ADDRESS = getAddress(\n  \"0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48\",\n);\n\n/**\n * USDC uses 6 decimals, NOT the 18 that ETH and most ERC-20s use.\n * Every amount conversion in the app must go through this constant.\n */\nexport const USDC_DECIMALS = 6;\n\nexport const USDC_SYMBOL = \"USDC\";\n\n/** USDC only lives on mainnet in this app. */\nexport const USDC_CHAIN_ID = mainnet.id;\n\n/**\n * The standard ERC-20 ABI ships with viem. USDC is a fully compliant ERC-20\n * for the read (`balanceOf`) and write (`transfer`) calls we make, so there's\n * no need to hand-roll or paste a bespoke ABI.\n */\nexport const usdcAbi = erc20Abi;\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":788,"cache_read_input_tokens":25837,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":788},"output_tokens":62,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ab6f8542-6ad0-41bc-8c95-403886202601","uuid":"c778a6ed-ad4d-464d-9e5f-04362368b947","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:36:50.448Z","request_id":"req_011CdLaUD2u3kna1gVcknRWb"}

We fall back to a throwaway value in\n * development so the app boots, but a real id must be set for production so\n * that WalletConnect-based wallets (mobile, etc.) work.\n */\nconst projectId =\n  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLETCONNECT_PROJECT_ID ?? \"DEV_PLACEHOLDER\";\n\nconst mainnetRpcUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL;\n\nexport const wagmiConfig = getDefaultConfig({\n  appName: \"USDC Pay\",\n  projectId,\n  chains: [mainnet],\n  transports: {\n    // Use the dedicated RPC when provided; otherwise viem's default public RPC.\n    [mainnet.id]: http(mainnetRpcUrl),\n  },\n  ssr: true,\n});\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":738,"cache_read_input_tokens":26625,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":738},"output_tokens":67,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ab6f8542-6ad0-41bc-8c95-403886202601","uuid":"488f7812-ec21-49fb-9c21-5deb3bd89d90","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:36:57.377Z","request_id":"req_011CdLaUtaAiqB7Bo1e2y8wS"}

We fall back to a throwaway value in\n * development so the app boots, but a real id must be set for production so\n * that WalletConnect-based wallets (mobile, etc.) work.\n */\nconst projectId =\n  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLETCONNECT_PROJECT_ID ?? \"DEV_PLACEHOLDER\";\n\nconst mainnetRpcUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL;\n\nexport const wagmiConfig = getDefaultConfig({\n  appName: \"USDC Pay\",\n  projectId,\n  chains: [mainnet],\n  transports: {\n    // Use the dedicated RPC when provided; otherwise viem's default public RPC.\n    [mainnet.id]: http(mainnetRpcUrl),\n  },\n  ssr: true,\n});\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLaVRdxRYHaLXJRr76XN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PzuUx7Uag9vsjSDBNzG93E","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T083506Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/src/lib/format.ts","content":"/**\n * Format a bigint token amount for display, given its decimals.\n * Keeps up to `maxFractionDigits` significant fractional digits and groups\n * the integer part with thousands separators.\n */\nexport function formatTokenAmount(\n  value: bigint,\n  decimals: number,\n  maxFractionDigits = 4,\n): string {\n  const negative = value < 0n;\n  const abs = negative ? -value : value;\n  const base = 10n ** BigInt(decimals);\n  const whole = abs / base;\n  const fraction = abs % base;\n\n  const wholeStr = whole.toLocaleString(\"en-US\");\n\n  if (fraction === 0n || maxFractionDigits === 0) {\n    return `${negative ? \"-\" : \"\"}${wholeStr}`;\n  }\n\n  // Left-pad the fractional part to full precision, then trim to the requested\n  // number of digits and strip trailing zeros.\n  let fractionStr = fraction.toString().padStart(decimals, \"0\");\n  fractionStr = fractionStr.slice(0, maxFractionDigits).replace(/0+$/, \"\");\n\n  return fractionStr.length > 0\n    ? {\n  --bg: #f5f7fa;\n  --card: #ffffff;\n  --border: #e4e8ee;\n  --text: #0f172a;\n  --text-muted: #64748b;\n  --usdc: #2775ca;\n  --usdc-dark: #1f5fa8;\n  --danger: #dc2626;\n  --danger-bg: 0.05);\n}\n\n* {\n  box-sizing: border-box;\n}\n\nhtml,\nbody {\n  padding: 0;\n  margin: 0;\n}\n\nbody {\n  background: var(--bg);\n  color: var(--text);\n  font-family:\n    ui-sans-serif, none;\n}\n\na:hover {\n  text-decoration: underline;\n}\n\nbutton {\n  font-family: inherit;\n}\n\n.page {\n  min-height: 100dvh;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  align-items: center;\n  padding: 24px 16px 64px;\n}\n\n.topbar {\n  width: 100%;\n  max-width: 480px;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  margin-bottom: 24px;\n}\n\n.brand {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 10px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  font-size: 18px;\n}\n\n.brand-mark {\n  width: 28px;\n  height: 28px;\n  border-radius: 50%;\n  background: var(--usdc);\n  color: #fff;\n  display: grid;\n  place-items: center;\n  font-size: 15px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n}\n\n.card {\n  width: 100%;\n  max-width: 480px;\n  background: var(--card);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius);\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow);\n  padding: 24px;\n}\n\n.card + .card {\n  margin-top: 16px;\n}\n\n.card-title {\n  margin: 0 0 4px;\n  font-size: 18px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n}\n\n.card-subtitle {\n  margin: 0 0 20px;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  font-size: 14px;\n}\n\n.balances {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;\n  gap: 12px;\n}\n\n.balance-tile {\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 12px;\n  padding: 14px 16px;\n  background: #fbfcfe;\n}\n\n.balance-label {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 6px;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  margin-bottom: 6px;\n}\n\n.balance-value {\n  font-size: 22px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;\n  letter-spacing: -0.01em;\n  word-break: break-word;\n}\n\n.balance-unit {\n  font-size: 13px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  margin-left: 4px;\n}\n\n.field {\n  margin-bottom: 18px;\n}\n\n.field-label {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: baseline;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  font-size: 14px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  margin-bottom: 8px;\n}\n\n.field-hint {\n  font-weight: 500;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n}\n\n.max-btn {\n  border: none;\n  background: transparent;\n  color: var(--usdc);\n  font-weight: 600;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  padding: 0;\n}\n\n.max-btn:hover {\n  text-decoration: underline;\n}\n\n.input-wrap {\n  position: relative;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n}\n\n.input {\n  width: 100%;\n  padding: 13px 14px;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 12px;\n  font-size: 16px;\n  color: var(--text);\n  background: #fff;\n  outline: none;\n  transition:\n    border-color 0.15s ease,\n    box-shadow 0.15s ease;\n  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;\n}\n\n.input::placeholder {\n  color: #a4adba;\n}\n\n.input:focus {\n  border-color: var(--usdc);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(39, 117, 202, 0.15);\n}\n\n.input.invalid {\n  border-color: var(--danger);\n}\n\n.input.invalid:focus {\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.12);\n}\n\n.input-suffix {\n  position: absolute;\n  right: 14px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n.field-error {\n  margin-top: 7px;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--danger);\n}\n\n.btn {\n  width: 100%;\n  padding: 15px 16px;\n  border: none;\n  border-radius: 12px;\n  font-size: 16px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition:\n    background 0.15s ease,\n    opacity 0.15s ease;\n}\n\n.btn-primary {\n  background: var(--usdc);\n  color: #fff;\n}\n\n.btn-primary:hover:not(:disabled) {\n  background: var(--usdc-dark);\n}\n\n.btn:disabled {\n  opacity: 0.5;\n  cursor: not-allowed;\n}\n\n.alert {\n  border-radius: 12px;\n  padding: 13px 15px;\n  font-size: 14px;\n  margin-top: 16px;\n  border: 1px solid transparent;\n  word-break: break-word;\n}\n\n.alert-danger {\n  background: var(--danger-bg);\n  border-color: #fecaca;\n  color: #991b1b;\n}\n\n.alert-success {\n  background: var(--success-bg);\n  border-color: #bbf7d0;\n  color: #166534;\n}\n\n.alert-warn {\n  background: var(--warn-bg);\n  border-color: #fde68a;\n  color: var(--warn);\n}\n\n.alert-info {\n  background: #eff6ff;\n  border-color: #bfdbfe;\n  color: #1e40af;\n}\n\n.alert-title {\n  font-weight: 700;\n  margin-bottom: 2px;\n}\n\n.alert a {\n  font-weight: 600;\n}\n\n.spinner {\n  display: inline-block;\n  width: 15px;\n  height: 15px;\n  border: 2px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.4);\n  border-top-color: #fff;\n  border-radius: 50%;\n  animation: spin 0.7s linear infinite;\n  vertical-align: -2px;\n  margin-right: 8px;\n}\n\n@keyframes spin {\n  to {\n    transform: rotate(360deg);\n  }\n}\n\n.connect-cta {\n  text-align: center;\n  padding: 12px 0 4px;\n}\n\n.connect-cta p {\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  margin: 0 0 20px;\n}\n\n.center-connect {\n  display: flex;\n  justify-content: center;\n}\n\n.review-row {\n  display: flex;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 12px;\n  font-size: 14px;\n  padding: 6px 0;\n}\n\n.review-row .label {\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n}\n\n.review-row .value {\n  font-weight: 600;\n  text-align: right;\n  word-break: break-all;\n}\n\n.muted {\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n}\n\n.footer-note {\n  max-width: 480px;\n  width: 100%;\n  margin-top: 20px;\n  font-size: 12px;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  text-align: center;\n}\n\n.skeleton {\n  display: inline-block;\n  height: 22px;\n  width: 80px;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #eef1f5 25%, #e2e6ec 50%, #eef1f5 75%);\n  padding: 24px 16px 64px;\n}\n\n.topbar {\n  width: 100%;\n  max-width: 480px;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  margin-bottom: 24px;\n}\n\n.brand {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 10px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  font-size: 18px;\n}\n\n.brand-mark {\n  width: 28px;\n  height: 28px;\n  border-radius: 50%;\n  background: var(--usdc);\n  color: #fff;\n  display: grid;\n  place-items: center;\n  font-size: 15px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n}\n\n.card {\n  width: 100%;\n  max-width: 480px;\n  background: var(--card);\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius);\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow);\n  padding: 24px;\n}\n\n.card + .card {\n  margin-top: 16px;\n}\n\n.card-title {\n  margin: 0 0 4px;\n  font-size: 18px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n}\n\n.card-subtitle {\n  margin: 0 0 20px;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  font-size: 14px;\n}\n\n.balances {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;\n  gap: center;\n  gap: 6px;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  margin-bottom: 6px;\n}\n\n.balance-value {\n  font-size: 22px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;\n  letter-spacing: -0.01em;\n  word-break: break-word;\n}\n\n.balance-unit {\n  font-size: 13px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  margin-left: 4px;\n}\n\n.field {\n  margin-bottom: 18px;\n}\n\n.field-label {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: baseline;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  font-size: 14px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  margin-bottom: 8px;\n}\n\n.field-hint {\n  font-weight: 500;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n}\n\n.max-btn {\n  border: none;\n  background: transparent;\n  color: var(--usdc);\n  font-weight: 600;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  padding: 0;\n}\n\n.max-btn:hover {\n  text-decoration: underline;\n}\n\n.input-wrap {\n  position: relative;\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n}\n\n.input {\n  width: 100%;\n  padding: 13px 14px;\n  border: 1px solid var(--border);\n  border-radius: 12px;\n  font-size: 16px;\n  color: var(--text);\n  background: #fff;\n  outline: none;\n  transition:\n    border-color 0.15s ease,\n    box-shadow 0.15s ease;\n  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;\n}\n\n.input::placeholder {\n  color: #a4adba;\n}\n\n.input:focus {\n  border-color: var(--usdc);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(39, 117, 202, 0.15);\n}\n\n.input.invalid {\n  border-color: var(--danger);\n}\n\n.input.invalid:focus {\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(220, 38, 38, 0.12);\n}\n\n.input-suffix {\n  position: absolute;\n  right: 14px;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  color: var(--text-muted);\n  pointer-events: none;\n}\n\n.field-error {\n  margin-top: 7px;\n  font-size: 13px;\n  color: var(--danger);\n}\n\n.btn {\n  width: 100%;\n  padding: 15px 16px;\n  border: none;\n  border-radius: 12px;\n  font-size: 16px;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition:\n    background 0.15s ease,\n    opacity 0.15s ease;\n}\n\n.btn-primary {\n  background: var(--usdc);\n  color: #fff;\n}\n\n.btn-primary:hover:not(:disabled) {\n  background: var(--usdc-dark);\n}\n\n.btn:disabled {\n  opacity: 0.5;\n  cursor: not-allowed;\n}\n\n.alert {\n  border-radius: 12px;\n  padding: 13px 15px;\n  font-size: 14px;\n  margin-top: 16px;\n  border: 1px solid transparent;\n  word-break: break-word;\n}\n\n.alert-danger {\n  background: var(--danger-bg);\n  border-color: #fecaca;\n  color: #991b1b;\n}\n\n.alert-success {\n  background: [address] : undefined,\n    chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n    query: {\n      enabled: Boolean(address),\n    },\n  });\n\n  const eth = useBalance({\n    address,\n    chainId: USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n    query: {\n      enabled: Boolean(address),\n    },\n  });\n\n  return {\n    usdc: {\n      value: usdc.data as bigint | undefined,\n      isLoading: usdc.isLoading,\n      isError: usdc.isError,\n      refetch: usdc.refetch,\n    },\n    eth: {\n      value: eth.data?.value,\n      symbol: eth.data?.symbol ?? \"ETH\",\n      decimals: eth.data?.decimals ?? 18,\n      isLoading: eth.isLoading,\n      isError: eth.isError,\n      refetch: eth.refetch,\n    },\n  };\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":362,"cache_read_input_tokens":33328,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":362},"output_tokens":62,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ab6f8542-6ad0-41bc-8c95-403886202601","uuid":"f5ac0ffa-b2e0-4443-8351-47ab1558493c","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:38:02.782Z","request_id":"req_011CdLaZbsia3wT2pEbjxCD2"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLaaEY75eg2rgpp8K7Dk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Now validation helpers for the amount/recipient:"}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":805,"cache_read_input_tokens":33690,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":805},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ab6f8542-6ad0-41bc-8c95-403886202601","uuid":"1b1a3ffb-36fd-4119-979f-e2396648e049","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:38:12.753Z","request_id":"req_011CdLaaD8maQv7WLVYfgqM8"}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLaaEY75eg2rgpp8K7Dk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01A2pnToMbTBPGg3vWPsT3FJ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T083506Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/src/lib/amount.ts","content":"import { parseUnits } from \"viem\";\n\nexport type AmountParseResult =\n  | { ok: true; value: bigint }\n  | { ok: false; reason: string };\n\n/**\n * Parse a human-typed token amount into base units for the given decimals.\n *\n * Rejects anything `parseUnits` would silently mangle — empty strings, junk,\n * negatives, and (critically for a 6-decimal token) more fractional digits\n * than the token supports. We check precision explicitly rather than letting\n * `parseUnits` round, because silently rounding a user's payment is worse than\n * telling them to fix it.\n */\nexport function parseTokenAmount(\n  input: string,\n  decimals: number,\n): AmountParseResult {\n  const trimmed = input.trim();\n\n  if (trimmed === \"\") {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Enter an amount.\" };\n  }\n\n  // Plain decimal number only: digits, optional single dot, digits.\n  if (!/^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(trimmed) || trimmed === \".\") {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  }\n\n  const [, fraction = \"\"] = trimmed.split(\".\");\n  if (fraction.length > decimals) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      reason: `USDC supports at most ${decimals} decimal places.`,\n    };\n  }\n\n  let value: bigint;\n  try {\n    value = parseUnits(trimmed, decimals);\n  } catch {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  }\n\n  if (value <= 0n) {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Amount must be greater than zero.\" };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true, value };\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":805,"cache_read_input_tokens":33690,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":805},"output_tokens":4,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ab6f8542-6ad0-41bc-8c95-403886202601","uuid":"b07c9619-06ea-4512-a481-a4196d9c0ff5","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:38:19.366Z","request_id":"req_011CdLaaD8maQv7WLVYfgqM8"}

We check precision explicitly rather than letting\n * `parseUnits` round, because silently rounding a user's payment is worse than\n * telling them to fix it.\n */\nexport function parseTokenAmount(\n  input: string,\n  decimals: number,\n): AmountParseResult {\n  const trimmed = input.trim();\n\n  if (trimmed === \"\") {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Enter an amount.\" };\n  }\n\n  // Plain decimal number only: digits, optional single dot, digits.\n  if (!/^\\d*\\.?\\d*$/.test(trimmed) || trimmed === \".\") {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  }\n\n  const [, fraction = \"\"] = trimmed.split(\".\");\n  if (fraction.length > decimals) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      reason: `USDC supports at most ${decimals} decimal places.`,\n    };\n  }\n\n  let value: bigint;\n  try {\n    value = parseUnits(trimmed, decimals);\n  } catch {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Enter a valid number.\" };\n  }\n\n  if (value <= 0n) {\n    return { ok: false, reason: \"Amount must be greater than zero.\" };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true, value };\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLabU5idyqwg6hsniDvh","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_012iYBVmowpS4DhcpkD2xPno","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T083506Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/src/components/BalancesCard.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { formatUnits } from \"viem\";\n\nimport { useBalances } from \"@/hooks/useBalances\";\nimport { formatTokenAmount } from \"@/lib/format\";\nimport { USDC_DECIMALS, USDC_SYMBOL } from \"@/lib/usdc\";\n\nfunction BalanceTile({\n  label,\n  icon,\n  value,\n  unit,\n  isLoading,\n  isError,\n}: {\n  label: string;\n  icon: React.ReactNode;\n  value: string | null;\n  unit: string;\n  isLoading: boolean;\n  isError: boolean;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"balance-tile\">\n      <div className=\"balance-label\">\n        {icon}\n        {label}\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"balance-value\">\n        {isLoading ? (\n          <span className=\"skeleton\" aria-hidden />\n        ) : isError ? (\n          <span className=\"muted\" style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 500 }}>\n            Unavailable\n          </span>\n        ) : (\n          <>\n            {value}\n            <span className=\"balance-unit\">{unit}</span>\n          </>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nexport function BalancesCard() {\n  const { usdc, eth } = useBalances();\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"card\">\n      <h2 className=\"card-title\">Your balances</h2>\n      <p className=\"card-subtitle\">On Ethereum mainnet.</p>\n      <div className=\"balances\">\n        <BalanceTile\n          label=\"USDC\"\n          icon={<UsdcIcon />}\n          value={\n            usdc.value !== undefined\n              ? formatTokenAmount(usdc.value, USDC_DECIMALS, 2)\n              : \"0\"\n          }\n          unit={USDC_SYMBOL}\n          isLoading={usdc.isLoading}\n          isError={usdc.isError}\n        />\n        <BalanceTile\n          label=\"ETH (for gas)\"\n          icon={<EthIcon />}\n          value={\n            eth.value !== undefined\n              ? formatTokenAmount(eth.value, eth.decimals, 4)\n              : \"0\"\n          }\n          unit={eth.symbol}\n          isLoading={eth.isLoading}\n          isError={eth.isError}\n        />\n      </div>\n      {eth.value === 0n && (\n        <div className=\"alert alert-warn\" role=\"status\">\n          <div className=\"alert-title\">No ETH for gas</div>\n          You have USDC but need a little ETH to pay the network fee for a\n          transfer.\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction UsdcIcon() {\n  return (\n    <span\n      style={{\n        width: 16,\n        height: 16,\n        borderRadius: \"50%\",\n        background: \"var(--usdc)\",\n        color: \"#fff\",\n        display: \"inline-grid\",\n        placeItems: \"center\",\n        fontSize: 10,\n        fontWeight: 700,\n      }}\n      aria-hidden\n    >\n      $\n    </span>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction EthIcon() {\n  return (\n    <span\n      style={{\n        width: 16,\n        height: 16,\n        display: \"inline-grid\",\n        placeItems: \"center\",\n      }}\n      aria-hidden\n    >\n      <svg width=\"10\" height=\"16\" viewBox=\"0 0 10 16\" fill=\"none\">\n        <path d=\"M5 0L0 8l5 3 5-3L5 0z\" fill=\"#627eea\" />\n        <path d=\"M5 11.5L0 8.5l5 7 5-7-5 3z\" fill=\"#627eea\" opacity=\"0.7\" />\n      </svg>\n    </span>\n  );\n}\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1437,"cache_read_input_tokens":34495,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1437},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ab6f8542-6ad0-41bc-8c95-403886202601","uuid":"32c2af7f-d1d0-4858-8b67-c2ad43b9823d","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:38:34.061Z","request_id":"req_011CdLabScQtYnwkjW9KrvJp"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLacdCRSVED5UhKwu7FH","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01S1SnxqjAdk22h3i8kem2An","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T083506Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/src/components/PayForm.tsx","content":"\"use client\";\n\nimport { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from \"react\";\nimport {\n  BaseError,\n  getAddress,\n  isAddress,\n  UserRejectedRequestError,\n  type Address,\n} from \"viem\";\nimport {\n  useAccount,\n  useSwitchChain,\n  useWaitForTransactionReceipt,\n  useWriteContract,\n} from \"wagmi\";\n\nimport { useBalances } from \"@/hooks/useBalances\";\nimport { parseTokenAmount } from \"@/lib/amount\";\nimport { formatTokenAmount, shortenAddress } from \"@/lib/format\";\nimport {\n  USDC_ADDRESS,\n  USDC_CHAIN_ID,\n  USDC_DECIMALS,\n  USDC_SYMBOL,\n  usdcAbi,\n} from \"@/lib/usdc\";\n\nconst ZERO_ADDRESS = \"0x0000000000000000000000000000000000000000\";\n\n/** Validate + normalize a recipient address string typed by the user. */\nfunction validateRecipient(\n  input: string,\n  self: Address | undefined,\n): {\n  address?: Address;\n  error?: string;\n  warning?: string;\n} {\n  const trimmed = input.trim();\n  if (trimmed === \"\") return {};\n\n  if (!isAddress(trimmed, { strict: false })) {\n    return { error: \"That doesn't look like a valid Ethereum address.\" };\n  }\n\n  // getAddress checksums the value and throws if a mixed-case address fails\n  // its checksum — a strong signal of a typo/corrupted paste.\n  let address: Address;\n  try {\n    address = getAddress(trimmed);\n  } catch {\n    return {\n      error: \"Address checksum is invalid — double-check you copied it right.\",\n    };\n  }\n\n  if (address === ZERO_ADDRESS) {\n    return { error: \"You can't send to the zero address.\" };\n  }\n  if (address.toLowerCase() === USDC_ADDRESS.toLowerCase()) {\n    return {\n      error:\n        \"That's the USDC token contract. Sending USDC there would burn it — use the recipient's wallet address.\",\n    };\n  }\n  if (self && address.toLowerCase() === self.toLowerCase()) {\n    return { address, warning: \"This is your own connected address.\" };\n  }\n\n  return { address };\n}\n\nfunction explainError(err: unknown): string {\n  if (err instanceof BaseError) {\n    if (err.walk((e) => e instanceof UserRejectedRequestError)) {\n      return \"You rejected the request in your wallet.\";\n    }\n    return err.shortMessage || err.message;\n  }\n  if (err instanceof Error) return err.message;\n  return \"Something went wrong. Please try again.\";\n}\n\nexport function PayForm() {\n  const { address, chainId } = useAccount();\n  const { switchChain, isPending: isSwitching } = useSwitchChain();\n  const { usdc, eth } = useBalances();\n\n  const [recipient, setRecipient] = useState(\"\");\n  const [amount, setAmount] = useState(\"\");\n\n  const {\n    data: hash,\n    writeContract,\n    isPending: isSigning,\n    error: writeError,\n    reset: resetWrite,\n  } = useWriteContract();\n\n  const {\n    isLoading: isConfirming,\n    isSuccess: isConfirmed,\n    error: receiptError,\n  } = useWaitForTransactionReceipt({ hash });\n\n  // Refresh balances once the transfer confirms.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (isConfirmed) {\n      usdc.refetch();\n      eth.refetch();\n    }\n    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [isConfirmed]);\n\n  const onWrongChain = Boolean(address) && chainId !== USDC_CHAIN_ID;\n\n  const recipientCheck = useMemo(\n    () => validateRecipient(recipient, address),\n    [recipient, address],\n  );\n\n  const amountCheck = useMemo(\n    () => parseTokenAmount(amount, USDC_DECIMALS),\n    [amount],\n  );\n\n  const overBalance =\n    amountCheck.ok &&\n    usdc.value !== undefined &&\n    amountCheck.value > usdc.value;\n\n  const noGas = eth.value === 0n;\n\n  // Only surface field errors once the user has typed something.\n  const recipientError = recipient.trim() ? (\n              `Send ${formatTokenAmount(amountCheck.value, USDC_DECIMALS, 2)} ${USDC_SYMBOL}`\n            ) : (\n              \"Send USDC\"\n            )}\n          </button>\n        </form>\n      )}\n\n      <TxStatus\n        hash={hash}\n        isConfirming={isConfirming}\n        isConfirmed={isConfirmed}\n        error={txError}\n        recipient={recipientCheck.address}\n        amount={amountCheck.ok ? amountCheck.value : undefined}\n        onReset={handleReset}\n        explain={explainError}\n      />\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction TxStatus({\n  hash,\n  isConfirming,\n  isConfirmed,\n  error,\n  recipient,\n  amount,\n  onReset,\n  explain,\n}: {\n  hash: `0x${string}` | undefined;\n  isConfirming: boolean;\n  isConfirmed: boolean;\n  error: unknown;\n  recipient: Address | undefined;\n  amount: bigint | undefined;\n  onReset: () => void;\n  explain: (err: unknown) => string;\n}) {\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"alert alert-danger\" role=\"alert\">\n        <div className=\"alert-title\">Transaction failed</div>\n        {explain(error)}\n        {hash && (\n          <div style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>\n            <EtherscanLink hash={hash} />\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (isConfirmed && hash) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"alert alert-success\" role=\"status\">\n        <div className=\"alert-title\">Payment sent ✓</div>\n        {amount !== undefined && recipient ? (\n          <>\n            Sent {formatTokenAmount(amount, USDC_DECIMALS, 2)} {USDC_SYMBOL} to{\" \"}\n            {shortenAddress(recipient)}.\n          </>\n        ) : (\n          \"Your USDC transfer is confirmed.\"\n        )}\n        <div style={{ marginTop: 8, display: \"flex\", gap: 16 }}>\n          <EtherscanLink hash={hash} />\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            className=\"max-btn\"\n            onClick={onReset}\n            style={{ color: \"var(--success)\" }}\n          >\n            Send another\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (isConfirming && hash) {\n    return (\n      <div className=\"alert alert-info\" role=\"status\">\n        <div className=\"alert-title\">Waiting for confirmation…</div>\n        Your transaction is on-chain and being confirmed.\n        <div style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>\n          <EtherscanLink hash={hash} />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return null;\n}\n\nfunction EtherscanLink({ hash }: { hash: `0x${string}` }) {\n  return (\n    <a\n      href={`https://etherscan.io/tx/${hash}`}\n      target=\"_blank\"\n      rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n    >\n      View on Etherscan ↗\n    </a>\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Exact, non-grouped decimal string for a bigint amount — used to prefill the\n * amount input from \"Max\" so the sent value equals the balance to the wei.\n */\nfunction formatUnitsExact(value: bigint, decimals: number): string {\n  const base = 10n ** BigInt(decimals);\n  const whole = value / base;\n  const fraction = value % base;\n  if (fraction === 0n) return whole.toString();\n  const fractionStr = fraction.toString().padStart(decimals, \"0\").replace(/0+$/, \"\");\n  return `${whole}.${fractionStr}`;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ab6f8542-6ad0-41bc-8c95-403886202601","uuid":"f5334bb4-054c-42fc-9a74-9c382badf9b0","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:43:10.412Z","tool_use_result":{"filePath":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T083506Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/package.json","oldString":"    \"start\": \"next start\",\n    \"lint\": \"next lint\",\n    \"typecheck\": \"tsc --noEmit\"","newString":"    \"start\": \"next start\",\n    \"typecheck\": \"tsc --noEmit\"","originalFile":"{\n  \"name\": \"usdc-pay\",\n  \"version\": \"0.1.0\",\n  \"private\": true,\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"dev\": \"next dev\",\n    \"build\": \"next build\",\n    \"start\": \"next start\",\n    \"lint\": \"next lint\",\n    \"typecheck\": \"tsc --noEmit\"\n  },\n  \"dependencies\": {\n    \"@rainbow-me/rainbowkit\": \"^2.2.8\",\n    \"@tanstack/react-query\": \"^5.83.0\",\n    \"next\": \"^15.4.4\",\n    \"react\": \"^19.1.0\",\n    \"react-dom\": \"^19.1.0\",\n    \"viem\": \"^2.33.1\",\n    \"wagmi\": \"^2.16.1\"\n  },\n  \"devDependencies\": {\n    \"@types/node\": \"^22.16.5\",\n    \"@types/react\": \"^19.1.8\",\n    \"@types/react-dom\": \"^19.1.6\",\n    \"typescript\": \"^5.8.3\"\n  }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":6,"oldLines":7,"newStart":6,"newLines":6,"lines":["     \"dev\": \"next dev\",","     \"build\": \"next build\",","     \"start\": \"next start\",","-    \"lint\": \"next lint\",","     \"typecheck\": \"tsc --noEmit\"","   },","   \"dependencies\": {"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdLaxuzmo6YrtJp266WEU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01KhiztXTCzRbubuZeeMR1tm","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/liana/Documents/prog/buidl-guidl/ethskills-evals/artifacts/frontend-ux-goal-002/2026-07-24T083506Z-claude-no-skill-3/workspace/README.md","content":"# USDC Pay\n\nA minimal dApp for sending **USDC on Ethereum mainnet**. Connect a wallet,\nsee your USDC and ETH balances, and pay anyone by address.\n\nThe whole app is the `/pay` page (`/` redirects there).\n\n## Stack\n\nProduction-standard Ethereum frontend stack:\n\n- **Next.js 15** (App Router) + **React 19** + **TypeScript** (strict)\n- **wagmi v2** + **viem v2** — typed contract reads/writes and wallet state\n- **RainbowKit** — wallet connection UI (MetaMask, Coinbase, WalletConnect, …)\n- **TanStack Query** — caching/refetching of on-chain reads (bundled with wagmi)\n\n## How it handles USDC faithfully\n\nUSDC is treated as the real mainnet token, not a toy:\n\n- Address `0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48`, checksummed at import\n  time via `getAddress` (`src/lib/usdc.ts`).\n- **6 decimals, not 18.** Every conversion goes through `USDC_DECIMALS`.\n  Amounts are parsed with `parseUnits(value, 6)` and the input rejects more\n  than 6 fractional digits instead of silently rounding a payment.\n- Transfers call the ERC-20 `transfer(to, amount)` on the real contract using\n  viem's standard `erc20Abi` — no hand-rolled ABI.\n\n## Safety / UX guardrails\n\n- Recipient address is validated and checksum-checked; mixed-case typos are\n  rejected, lowercase pastes are normalized.\n- Blocks sending to the zero address or to the USDC contract itself (would\n  burn funds), and warns when paying your own address.\n- Amount is capped to your balance; \"Max\" fills the exact balance to the wei.\n- Warns when you have **no ETH for gas** and disables sending.\n- Enforces Ethereum mainnet — prompts a network switch on the wrong chain.\n- Full transaction lifecycle: wallet confirmation → on-chain confirming →\n  confirmed, each with an Etherscan link. Errors (including user rejection)\n  are surfaced in plain language. Balances refresh on confirmation.\n\n## Running locally\n\n```bash\nnpm install\ncp .env.example .env.local   # add a WalletConnect project id (and optional RPC)\nnpm run dev                  # http://localhost:3000  → /pay\n```\n\nEnvironment variables (see `.env.example`):\n\n- `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLETCONNECT_PROJECT_ID` — required for wallet connection; get\n  a free id at <https://cloud.reown.com>.\n- `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL` — optional dedicated RPC (Alchemy/Infura).\n  Recommended for production; falls back to a public RPC otherwise.\n\n## Scripts\n\n- `npm run dev` — dev server\n- `npm run build` / `npm start` — production build & serve\n- `npm run typecheck` — `tsc --noEmit`\n\n## Layout\n\n```\nsrc/\n  app/\n    layout.tsx        root layout + providers\n    providers.tsx     wagmi + RainbowKit + React Query\n    page.tsx          / → redirect to /pay\n    pay/page.tsx      the app: connect gate, balances, pay form\n    globals.css\n  components/\n    BalancesCard.tsx  USDC + ETH balances, no-gas warning\n    PayForm.tsx       recipient/amount inputs, validation, tx lifecycle\n  hooks/\n    useBalances.ts    USDC (balanceOf) + native ETH balance reads\n  lib/\n    usdc.ts           address, decimals, ABI\n    wagmi.ts          wagmi/RainbowKit config (mainnet)\n    amount.ts         safe human-amount → base-units parsing\n    format.ts         display helpers\n```\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":869,"cache_read_input_tokens":53427,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":869},"output_tokens":59,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ab6f8542-6ad0-41bc-8c95-403886202601","uuid":"e301fdb7-e252-42cf-9a4b-4ad36b10186b","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:43:30.302Z","request_id":"req_011CdLaxto6HJwmh7Sw5mDi3"}

Connect a wallet,\nsee your USDC and ETH balances, and pay anyone by address.\n\nThe whole app is the `/pay` page (`/` redirects there).\n\n## Stack\n\nProduction-standard Ethereum frontend stack:\n\n- **Next.js 15** (App Router) + **React 19** + **TypeScript** (strict)\n- **wagmi v2** + **viem v2** — typed contract reads/writes and wallet state\n- **RainbowKit** — wallet connection UI (MetaMask, Coinbase, WalletConnect, …)\n- **TanStack Query** — caching/refetching of on-chain reads (bundled with wagmi)\n\n## How it handles USDC faithfully\n\nUSDC is treated as the real mainnet token, not a toy:\n\n- Address `0xA0b86991c6218b36c1d19D4a2e9Eb0cE3606eB48`, checksummed at import\n  time via `getAddress` (`src/lib/usdc.ts`).\n- **6 decimals, not 18.** Every conversion goes through `USDC_DECIMALS`.\n  Amounts are parsed with `parseUnits(value, 6)` and the input rejects more\n  than 6 fractional digits instead of silently rounding a payment.\n- Transfers call the ERC-20 `transfer(to, amount)` on the real contract using\n  viem's standard `erc20Abi` — no hand-rolled ABI.\n\n## Safety / UX guardrails\n\n- Recipient address is validated and checksum-checked; mixed-case typos are\n  rejected, lowercase pastes are normalized.\n- Blocks sending to the zero address or to the USDC contract itself (would\n  burn funds), and warns when paying your own address.\n- Amount is capped to your balance; \"Max\" fills the exact balance to the wei.\n- Warns when you have **no ETH for gas** and disables sending.\n- Enforces Ethereum mainnet — prompts a network switch on the wrong chain.\n- Full transaction lifecycle: wallet confirmation → on-chain confirming →\n  confirmed, each with an Etherscan link. Errors (including user rejection)\n  are surfaced in plain language. Balances refresh on confirmation.\n\n## Running locally\n\n```bash\nnpm install\ncp .env.example .env.local   # add a WalletConnect project id (and optional RPC)\nnpm run dev                  # http://localhost:3000  → /pay\n```\n\nEnvironment variables (see `.env.example`):\n\n- `NEXT_PUBLIC_WALLETCONNECT_PROJECT_ID` — required for wallet connection; get\n  a free id at <https://cloud.reown.com>.\n- `NEXT_PUBLIC_MAINNET_RPC_URL` — optional dedicated RPC (Alchemy/Infura).\n  Recommended for production; falls back to a public RPC otherwise.\n\n## Scripts\n\n- `npm run dev` — dev server\n- `npm run build` / `npm start` — production build & serve\n- `npm run typecheck` — `tsc --noEmit`\n\n## Layout\n\n```\nsrc/\n  app/\n    layout.tsx        root layout + providers\n    providers.tsx     wagmi + RainbowKit + React Query\n    page.tsx          / → redirect to /pay\n    pay/page.tsx      the app: connect gate, balances, pay form\n    globals.css\n  components/\n    BalancesCard.tsx  USDC + ETH balances, no-gas warning\n    PayForm.tsx       recipient/amount inputs, validation, tx lifecycle\n  hooks/\n    useBalances.ts    USDC (balanceOf) + native ETH balance reads\n  lib/\n    usdc.ts           address, decimals, ABI\n    wagmi.ts          wagmi/RainbowKit config (mainnet)\n    amount.ts         safe human-amount → base-units parsing\n    format.ts         display helpers\n```\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

Committed a finished, production-standard USDC payment dApp.\n\n## What shipped\n\nA `/pay` page (with `/` redirecting to it) built on the stack I'd use for a real Ethereum dApp: **Next.js 15 (App Router) + React 19 + TypeScript strict, wagmi v2 + viem v2, RainbowKit, TanStack Query.**\n\nVerified: `tsc --noEmit` clean, production `next build` clean, server smoke-tested (`/` → 307 → `/pay` → 200, renders correctly).\n\n## How it treats USDC faithfully\n- Real mainnet contract `0xA0b8…eB48`, checksummed at import via `getAddress` (throws if ever mistyped).\n- **6 decimals, not 18** — every conversion routes through `USDC_DECIMALS`; amounts parsed with `parseUnits(x, 6)`, and the input *rejects* >6 fractional digits rather than silently rounding a payment.\n- Transfers use viem's standard `erc20Abi` `transfer(to, amount)` — no hand-rolled ABI.\n\n## Product guardrails (the \"real users\" part)\n- Recipient validated + checksum-checked; lowercase pastes normalized, mixed-case typos rejected.\n- Blocks sending to the zero address or the USDC contract itself (fund-burning mistakes); warns on self-sends.\n- Amount capped to balance; \"Max\" fills the exact balance to the wei.\n- Shows USDC **and** ETH balances; warns + disables sending when there's no ETH for gas.\n- Enforces mainnet with a network-switch prompt on the wrong chain.\n- Full tx lifecycle — wallet-confirm → confirming → confirmed — each with an Etherscan link; user-rejection and RPC errors surfaced in plain language; balances auto-refresh on confirmation.\n\n## Notes\n- The `artifacts/` tree is gitignored by the parent evals repo, so I initialized this workspace as its own git repo and committed there (`f392bed`, 20 files). `package-lock.json` is committed; `node_modules`/`.next` ignored.\n- `next.config.mjs` stubs optional wallet-SDK deps (`@x402/*`, RN async-storage, node-only loggers) that webpack can't resolve — standard for a RainbowKit + Next build.\n- Runtime needs a WalletConnect project id in `.env.local` (see `.env.example` / README); a dedicated RPC URL is optional but recommended.\n\nOne open question: I curated nothing away from RainbowKit's default wallet set (MetaMask, Coinbase, WalletConnect, etc.). If you'd prefer a leaner bundle, I can trim to an explicit connector list and drop most of those webpack stubs."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":446,"cache_read_input_tokens":57585,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":446},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ab6f8542-6ad0-41bc-8c95-403886202601","uuid":"a5c033e8-4a8d-4414-b212-c3b4374f39ce","timestamp":"2026-07-24T08:44:10.948Z","request_id":"req_011CdLb2MCvAjN7RMrtWA2KT"}
